# Boost B2 Frontier Replay Tracker

This branch replays the Boost B2 frontier work from the pre-WIP base instead
of keeping the first mixed snapshot as final history.

Safety copy:

- `safety/boost-b2-frontier-wip-20260506`
- snapshot commit: `48a77162`

Replay rule:

1. Restore the next smallest regression source into the earliest owning PA.
2. Number that regression inside the PA's existing semantic cluster
   (`400`, `500`, etc.) by matching nearby tests, not by assigning a new
   one-off sequence number.
3. Run that regression before the implementation fix and record the failing
   symptom.
4. Restore only the corresponding implementation change.
5. Validate the focused regression and the appropriate owner target.
6. Commit that coherent batch before moving to the next unrelated failure.

Known strict fallback exceptions from the exploratory Boost run remain outside
this replay unless reduced into a normal owner regression.

Local Boost B2 configuration overrides that are not compiler-fix ledger items:

- `BOOST_HAS_CLOCK_GETTIME`: host clang also compiles Boost.Config's negative
  probe on this macOS SDK because `clock_gettime` is available but Boost's
  macOS POSIX macro path does not define the feature macro.
- `BOOST_HAS_PRAGMA_DETECT_MISMATCH`: both host clang and cppgm compile the
  negative probe because an unsupported `#pragma detect_mismatch` is ignored or
  warned, not made fatal by default.
- `CPPGM_BOOST_B2_FRONTIER` local Boost.Config suffix hook for
  `BOOST_NO_CXX17_HDR_STRING_VIEW`: this libc++ exposes `<string_view>` in
  C++11 mode as an extension, so host clang and cppgm both compile the negative
  `BOOST_NO_CXX17_HDR_STRING_VIEW` probe even though Boost marks the header as
  unavailable from `__cplusplus`; the Boost-only hook clears only that Boost
  defect macro after the normal suffix logic when `__has_include(<string_view>)`
  succeeds.
- `CPPGM_BOOST_B2_FRONTIER` local Boost.Config suffix hook for
  `BOOST_NO_CXX98_FUNCTION_BASE`: libc++ 22 keeps `std::unary_function` and
  `std::binary_function` available, deprecated, in C++11/C++14 and removes them
  in C++17+, while Boost's libc++ config defines the defect macro for all
  C++11+ modes; the Boost-only hook clears that macro only for libc++ before
  C++17 so Boost.Config's C++11 compile-fail probe matches host clang.
- `__cpp_if_constexpr=201606L`: both host clang and cppgm accept `if constexpr`
  in C++11 mode as a warning-only extension, so Boost.Config's negative
  `BOOST_NO_CXX17_IF_CONSTEXPR` probe compiles despite Boost marking the feature
  unavailable from the absent SD-6 macro; the Boost-only config advertises the
  extension so the negative probe selects Boost's expected `#error` branch.
- `__cpp_inline_variables=201606L` plus the `CPPGM_BOOST_B2_FRONTIER` hook in
  Boost's local `boost/config/user.hpp`, which selects wrapper
  `cppgm-boost-config/compiler.hpp`: both host clang and cppgm accept inline
  variables in C++11 mode as a warning-only extension; Boost's clang config
  otherwise defines `BOOST_NO_CXX17_INLINE_VARIABLES` from `__cplusplus`, so the
  wrapper keeps the clang config and only clears that defect macro when the
  Boost-local config advertises the extension.
- `CPPGM_BOOST_B2_FRONTIER` local patch to
  `libs/config/test/no_ded_typename_fail.cpp`: Boost 1.91's fail probe is
  inverted because `BOOST_DEDUCED_TYPENAME` is always defined by
  `boost/config/detail/suffix.hpp`; forcing `BOOST_NO_DEDUCED_TYPENAME` before
  `boost/config.hpp` makes the local fail probe match host clang and exercise
  the missing-`typename` surface.
- `CPPGM_BOOST_B2_FRONTIER` local Boost.Config suffix hook for
  `BOOST_NO_CXX11_HDR_INITIALIZER_LIST`: cppgm now reports Clang's
  `cxx_generalized_initializers` feature query for the syntax support Boost
  uses in `BOOST_NO_CXX11_UNIFIED_INITIALIZATION_SYNTAX`, but the current
  hosted `<initializer_list>` surface still fails Boost.Config's header probe;
  the Boost-only hook keeps the header defect macro defined while leaving the
  syntax macro controlled by the compiler feature query.
- `cppgm-b2-toolchain.sh` wrapper in the Boost tree: Boost.Config's project
  target includes a C-only `config_test_c.c` compile action, while `cppgm++`
  is a C++ compiler. Boost.Build also runs compile/preprocess actions and link
  actions as separate tool invocations, and `config_link_test` builds a shared
  support library even when the outer test is requested with `link=static`.
  The wrapper routes C compile/preprocess actions to host clang, C++ compile
  and preprocess actions to the replay worktree `cppgm++`, and link actions to
  host clang++.

| fixed | Boost.Config `BOOST_NO_COMPLETE_VALUE_INITIALIZATION` array subobject check | cv-compatible array types were compared only at the array node, missing cv applied to nested element types; then `const` member-object access used `make_cv` instead of the array-aware `apply_cv`, so array members in `const` methods did not get the same element-qualified type representation as declarators | PA12/PA15 | `pa12/tests/spec/346-multidimensional-array-const-reference-binding.t`; `pa15/tests/spec/308-const-method-array-member-binds-const-reference.t` | PA12 reduced local array call and PA15 const-method member call both failed with `no viable overload` for `const matrix&`; direct Boost.Config failed at `failed_to_value_initialized(m_2d_char_array, "m_2d_char_array")` | `make -C pa12 check TEST=tests/spec/346-multidimensional-array-const-reference-binding.t CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; `make -C pa15 check TEST=tests/spec/308-const-method-array-member-binds-const-reference.t CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; `make -C pa12 test CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; `make -C pa15 test CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; guards: PA12 `337`, `340`, PA15 `304`; direct Boost.Config `config_test.cpp` advanced to separate `boost_no_pointer_to_member_const` invalid member-initializer failure | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Config `BOOST_NO_POINTER_TO_MEMBER_CONST` wrapper constructor | class member collection treated a nested pointer-to-member-function data-member declarator as method syntax and used the method-filtered declarator, stripping the trailing function `const` from the stored field type while the constructor parameter kept it | PA15 | `pa15/tests/spec/309-const-member-function-pointer-field-initializer.t` | reduced construction failed with `ERROR: invalid member initializer` before the parse-context fix | `make -C pa15 check TEST=tests/spec/309-const-member-function-pointer-field-initializer.t CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; guards: PA15 `307`, `261`, `262`, `270`; `make -C pa15 test CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; direct Boost.Config `config_test.cpp` advanced to separate libc++ `<regex>` `unknown function std::next` failure | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Config libc++ `<regex>` `std::next` call | function-template deduction resolved `typename iterator_traits<I>::difference_type` to `long int` after deducing `I`, then still compared that non-deduced parameter against an `int` argument instead of using the saved dependent qualified type text to skip deduction and let overload conversion rank it | PA22 | `pa22/tests/spec/507-nondeduced-qualified-member-type-allows-conversion.t` | reduced regression and direct Boost.Config failed with `unknown function ...::next`; trace showed candidate `next` dropped by `deduction-failed ... pattern=long int actual=int` | `make -C pa22 check TEST=tests/spec/507-nondeduced-qualified-member-type-allows-conversion.t CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; focused witness compare for `507`; guards: PA22 `119`, `117`, `463`, `468`; `make -C pa22 test-strict CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; direct Boost.Config `config_test.cpp` advanced to separate `BOOST_NO_DEPENDENT_TYPES_IN_TEMPLATE_VALUE_PARAMETERS` size failure | same commit |

| fixed | Boost.Charconv duplicate `num_digits<unsigned int>` and `num_digits<unsigned long long>` definitions while linking `roundtrip` | explicit function-template specialization metadata used the outer `template<>` declaration as the definition node, so linkage analysis missed the `inline` specifier on the inner function definition and emitted inline explicit specializations as strong external symbols | PA19 | `pa19/tests/spec/228-inline-explicit-function-specialization-weak.t`; guards `pa19/tests/spec/077-function-explicit-specialization-parameter-name.t`, `pa19/tests/spec/055-function-explicit-specialization-simple.t` | minimal reducer emitted `template<> inline int f<unsigned>(unsigned)` as `[binding=strong]`; after binding the specialization to the inner definition, direct Boost `num_digits` reducers and the compiled Boost objects emit weak symbols, matching the host compiler surface | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; focused PA19 checks for `228`, `077`, and `055`; `make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa19 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=4 ORDERED=true CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1` passed `128 / 128`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make test-strict-nobuild STRICT_PAS='pa19' STRICT_SUBTEST_JOBS=4 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1` compared `121` with zero failures and skipped `7`; forced Boost.Charconv B2 passes `quick`, `from_chars`, and `to_chars`, links and runs `roundtrip`, then advances to a runtime frontier in `test_roundtrip_integers` and floating round-trip checks before exit `139`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `+0.40%`, RSS `-0.96%`, footprint `-0.42%` | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Charconv `roundtrip` int128 reduction while investigating `test_roundtrip_integers` runtime failures | native i128 lowering used allocatable `r8`/`r9` as hidden carry/compare scratch registers, so a live call argument already placed in `r8` could be clobbered while materializing a later `__uint128_t` argument; separately, int128 constexpr arithmetic was folded into 64-bit literal nodes, truncating Boost's `digits_39` threshold used by `num_digits(boost::uint128_type)` | PA14/PA20/PA30 | `pa14/tests/spec/234-u128-constant-runtime-init-not-truncated.t`; `pa20/tests/spec/458-local-u128-constexpr-not-truncated.t`; `pa30/tests/spec/324-runtime-u128-call-arg-carry-scratch.t` | direct probes reproduced a lost `"u128-hi"` label after `UINT64_MAX + 12346` call-argument materialization and `num_digits((1 << 64) + 12345)` returning `39`; after the fix, i128 operation scratch uses backend-only registers and int128 constant values lower through runtime u128 operations instead of truncated LowIR literals | `make -C dev cppgm++ lowir2native -j4`; focused PA14, PA20, and PA30 checks passed; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa14 ...` passed `52 / 52`; same for PA20 passed `58 / 58`; same for PA30 passed `56 / 56`; `test-strict-nobuild` is unavailable for these PAs; forced Boost.Charconv B2 still passes `quick`, `from_chars`, and `to_chars` and remains at a separate `roundtrip` runtime frontier with corrupted integer buffers/floating mismatches before exit `139`; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `+0.27%`, RSS `+0.79%`, footprint `-0.38%` | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Charconv `roundtrip` signed int128 base-3 integer corruption | register allocation did not model helper-backed i128 binary operations as calls, so live caller-saved temporaries such as the `digit_table` pointer could be allocated in registers clobbered by `___umodti3` / `___udivti3` while the LowIR instruction itself was not a visible call | PA30 | `pa30/tests/spec/325-runtime-u128-helper-call-preserves-live-temp.t` | reduced loop printed a buffer of NULs instead of the expected base-3 digits because the table pointer in `r8` was clobbered across the hidden i128 modulo helper call; the Boost.Charconv integer-only probe failed at the first signed int128 case in base 3 | `make -C dev cppgm++ lowir2native -j4`; `make -C pa30 check TEST=tests/spec/325-runtime-u128-helper-call-preserves-live-temp.t CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa30 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=4 ORDERED=true CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1` passed `57 / 57`; forced Boost.Charconv B2 now passes `quick`, `from_chars`, and `to_chars`, and `roundtrip` advances past the integer failures to floating precision mismatches; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `-0.04%`, RSS `+1.35%`, footprint `-0.44%` | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Charconv `roundtrip` fixed-decimal float parsing | native lowering emitted large i64 constants as immediates for `add` / `sub`, but x86-64 ALU immediates are sign-extended imm32; the object backend therefore truncated constants such as `0x3030303030303030` in Boost.FastFloat's eight-digit parser and corrupted the parsed mantissa | PA23 | `pa23/tests/structural/867-runtime-i64-large-alu-immediate.t` | direct parser reducer returned mantissa `13133281767278669091` instead of `9497883`, and the core eight-digit expression returned `1618` for `"00000000"`; Boost.Charconv `roundtrip` parsed fixed decimal strings such as `0.00000000000000000009497883` as unrelated float values | `make -C dev cppgm++ lowir2native -j4`; focused PA23 check for `867`; `make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa23 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=4 ORDERED=true CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1` passed `98 / 98`; direct Boost.FastFloat parser and direct cppgm-built `from_chars.cpp` repro passed; forced Boost.Charconv B2 passes `quick`, `from_chars`, `to_chars`, `roundtrip`, and `limits`, then advances to separate `to_chars_sprintf` reference-buffer corruption; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `+0.09%`, RSS `+0.62%`, footprint `-0.39%` | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Charconv `to_chars_sprintf` decimal `snprintf(float, ...)` reference corruption | C++ default argument promotions were not applied to source ellipsis arguments, so `float` varargs reached LowIR as `f32`; after promotion to `double`, host variadic calls still missed the SysV/Darwin `%al` XMM-argument count because machine-IR liveness did not model that hidden call input | PA14/PA32 | `pa14/tests/spec/235-variadic-float-argument-promotes-to-double.t`; `pa32/tests/spec/232-host-variadic-float-call-abi.t` | minimal `snprintf(buffer, 64, "%.9g", float_value)` returned the wrong buffer before the fix; the LowIR reducer showed `call ... %t5` with `f32` before the PA14 fix, and the host-linked object still failed until the variadic call preserved `mov rax, 1` before `_snprintf` | `make -C dev cppgm++ lowir2native -j4`; focused PA14/PA32 checks passed; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa14 ...` passed `53 / 53`; PA23 report passed `98 / 98`; PA32 report passed `59 / 59`; forced Boost.Charconv B2 passes `quick`, `from_chars`, `to_chars`, `roundtrip`, and `limits`, then reaches the separate `to_chars_sprintf` hex-format reference frontier; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `+0.05%`, RSS `+0.01%`, footprint `-0.43%` | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Charconv `to_chars_sprintf` hex reference path using `std::hexfloat` on `std::stringstream` | hidden virtual-base arguments for member calls on a base subobject double-adjusted the virtual-base pointer: the callee requested its own layout offset while the object-argument virtual-base layout was already rooted in the most-derived object, so stream manipulators set flags on the wrong `ios_base` subobject | PA29 | `pa29/tests/spec/152-virtual-base-manipulator-hidden-argument.t` | non-STL reducer failed by calling a base member through a non-primary base with a virtual `IOS` base and then reading the most-derived virtual base unchanged; the hosted probe `std::stringstream ss; ss << std::hexfloat;` previously left the visible stream flags unchanged, which made Boost compare hex output against a decimal `stringstream` reference | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; focused PA29 check passed; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa29 ...` passed `8 / 8`; PA32 guards `205` and `206` passed; PA33 guards `232` and `251` passed; hosted `std::hexfloat` probes passed; forced Boost.Charconv B2 passed through `to_chars_sprintf`, `test_num_digits`, 128-bit tests, and float compute/parser tests, then advanced to a separate `from_chars_float` scalar braced-init-list frontier; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `+0.22%`, RSS `+0.35%`, footprint `-0.38%` | same commit |

| fixed | Boost.Algorithm `hex_test2` libc++ `std::deque<char>::__append_with_size` body parameter `__f` | output-side function parameter alias recovery preferred the member-template declaration parameter clause over the out-of-class definition parameter clause for hosted header-emitted definitions; libc++ declares `__append_with_size(_InputIterator __from, size_type __n)` but defines it with `_InputIterator __f`, so the emitted function body scope bound only `__from` and failed when analyzing `++__f` | PA35 | `pa35/tests/link/775-hosted-deque-assign-param-alias-runtime-smoke.t` | tiny hosted repro `std::deque<char> values; values.assign(text, text + 3);` failed with `unknown id-expression __f`; Boost B2 `libs/algorithm/test//hex_test2` failed at `/include/c++/v1/deque:1914` in the same instantiated body | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; `CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 make -C pa35 check TEST=tests/link/775-hosted-deque-assign-param-alias-runtime-smoke.t`; forced Boost B2 `libs/algorithm/test//hex_test2` passed; `CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa35 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=4 ORDERED=true` passed `113 / 113`; Algorithm target replay has advanced through `hex_test2` with stale-clean forced targets; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `-0.17%`, RSS `-1.93%`, footprint `+0.26%` | same commit |
| fixed | Boost.Algorithm `hex_test3` `std::ostream_iterator<char, char>` writing into `std::basic_ostringstream<char>` | hidden virtual-base arguments for a polymorphic base pointer dereference fell back to the static base-class virtual-base offset; for an object whose dynamic type places the shared virtual base at a different offset, such as `basic_ostringstream` viewed through `basic_ostream*`, the forwarded `basic_ios` pointer was wrong and `operator<<` eventually dereferenced an invalid stream buffer path | PA29 | `pa29/tests/spec/157-polymorphic-pointer-virtual-base-argument.t` | focused non-STL reducer stores a `B*` to a `D` object, then calls `read_value(*p)` where `read_value(B&)` needs the hidden `V` virtual-base pointer; before the fix it forwarded `p + 16` instead of loading the offset from the object's vtable, and the hosted `ostream_iterator` reducer crashed at `basic_streambuf::sputn` address `0x60` | `make -C dev cppgm++ -j4`; `CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 make -C pa29 check TEST=tests/spec/157-polymorphic-pointer-virtual-base-argument.t`; `CPPGM_LOWIR_DIRECT_TEXT_COMPARE=1 CPPGM_SKIP_DEV_REBUILD=1 make test-report-nobuild ACTIVE_TEST_REPORT_PAS=pa29 TEST_REPORT_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=1 TEST_REPORT_SUBTEST_JOBS=4 ORDERED=true` passed `13 / 13`; forced Boost B2 `libs/algorithm/test//hex_test3` passed; Algorithm target replay has advanced through `hex_test3` with stale-clean forced targets; perf check against `/tmp/cppgm-perf-after-merge-c24ec8a0-baseline-extracted.json` passed: instructions `-0.26%`, RSS `-0.06%`, footprint `-0.26%` | same commit |
